│ str_replac… │ ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────
│             │ │ {'command': 'create', 'path':                  │ File created
│             │ │ '/home/ubuntu/OmniPerf-Bench/perf-agents-benc… │ /home/ubuntu/
│             │ │ 'file_text': '# Token IDs CPU Copy             │
│             │ │ Optimization\n\n## Summary\n\nThis             │
│             │ │ optimization improves the performance of the   │
│             │ │ `condense()` operation in `InputBatch` by      │
│             │ │ reducing unnecessary memory copies when moving │
│             │ │ request data to fill empty slots.\n\n##        │
│             │ │ Problem\n\nThe original implementation in      │
│             │ │ `vllm/v1/worker/gpu_input_batch.py` copied the │
│             │ │ entire row of `token_ids_cpu` when condensing  │
│             │ │ the batch:\n\n```python\nself.token_ids_cpu =  │
│             │ │ self.token_ids_cpu\n```\n\nThis was            │
│             │ │ inefficient because:\n- `token_ids_cpu` has    │
│             │ │ shape `(max_num_reqs, max_model_len)` where    │
│             │ │ `max_model_len` can be 8192 or larger\n- Each  │
│             │ │ row is 32 KB (8192 * 4 bytes)\n- But actual    │
│             │ │ token counts per request are typically much    │
│             │ │ smaller (e.g., 150 tokens = 0.59 KB)\n- This   │
│             │ │ means ~98% of the copied data was unused       │
│             │ │ padding\n\n## Solution\n\nThe optimization     │
│             │ │ tracks the actual number of tokens per request │
│             │ │ and only copies that amount:\n\n1. Added       │
│             │ │ `num_tokens` array to track total tokens       │
│             │ │ (prompt + output) per request\n2. Updated      │
│             │ │ `add_request()` to initialize `num_tokens`\n3. │
│             │ │ Updated `condense()` to copy only `num_tokens` │
│             │ │ elements\n4. Updated `gpu_model_runner.py` to  │
│             │ │ maintain `num_tokens` when appending new       │
│             │ │ tokens\n\n## Changes\n\n###                    │
│             │ │ vllm/v1/worker/gpu_input_batch.py\n\n1. Added  │
│             │ │ `num_tokens` array                             │
│             │ │ initialization:\n```python\nself.num_tokens =  │
│             │ │ np.zeros(max_num_reqs,                         │
│             │ │ dtype=np.int32)\n```\n\n2. Updated             │
│             │ │ `add_request()` to track total                 │
│             │ │ tokens:\n```python\nnum_output_tokens =        │
│             │ │ len(request.output_token_ids)\nself.num_tokens │
│             │ │ = num_prompt_tokens +                          │
│             │ │ num_output_tokens\n```\n\n3. Optimized         │
│             │ │ `condense()` to copy only actual               │
│             │ │ tokens:\n```python\nnum_tokens_to_copy =       │
│             │ │ self.num_tokens\nself.token_ids_cpu = \\\n     │
│             │ │ self.token_ids_cpu\nself.num_tokens =          │
│             │ │ self.num_tokens\n```\n\n###                    │
│             │ │ vllm/v1/worker/gpu_model_runner.py\n\nUpdated  │
│             │ │ the sampling loop to maintain                  │
│             │ │ `num_tokens`:\n```python\nself.input_batch.nu… │
│             │ │ = seq_len + 1\n```\n\n## Performance           │
│             │ │ Impact\n\nBased on testing with realistic      │
│             │ │ parameters:\n- **Configuration**: 100          │
│             │ │ requests, 100 prompt tokens + 50 output tokens │
│             │ │ per request\n- **Memory per row**: 32 KB       │
│             │ │ (full) vs 0.59 KB (actual)\n- **Copy size      │
│             │ │ reduction**: 54.61x\n- **Condense speedup**:   │
│             │ │ ~54.61x\n- **Time saved per condense**: ~8.37  │
│             │ │ ms\n\n## Testing\n\n### Correctness            │
│             │ │ Test\nCreated `test_condense_correctness.py`   │
│             │ │ to verify:\n- Token counts are correctly       │
│             │ │ tracked\n- Data is preserved after condense    │
│             │ │ operations\n- Request indices are correctly    │
│             │ │ updated\n\n### Performance Test\nCreated       │
│             │ │ `test_performance.py` to measure:\n- Single    │
│             │ │ condense operation time\n- Memory copy         │
│             │ │ reduction ratio\n- Expected throughput         │
│             │ │ improvement\n\n### Existing Tests\nThe         │
│             │ │ existing test in                               │
│             │ │ `tests/v1/worker/test_gpu_input_batch.py`      │
│             │ │ should continue to pass, as the optimization   │
│             │ │ maintains functional equivalence.\n\n##        │
│             │ │ Impact\n\nThis optimization significantly      │
│             │ │ reduces CPU overhead in the request batching   │
│             │ │ pipeline, especially for:\n- Large batch       │
│             │ │ sizes\n- Long context windows\n-               │
│             │ │ High-throughput inference scenarios\n\nThe     │
│             │ │ improvement is particularly valuable in        │
│             │ │ production deployments where condense          │
│             │ │ operations happen frequently.\n'}              │
